| D-266 | 2026-09-10 | **P166: fascism separated from totalitarianism, and tempo put in the definition** | **Author instruction**: Arendt's unit is the totalitarian state, conservative, wanting the bureaucracy to hold; fascism's mark is the line of abolition, acceleration toward destruction including its own; the fourth feature is the symptom and the tempo is the thing; and this makes §5.1.3 structural rather than incidental. **New run-in in §2.1.2, *Fascism is not totalitarianism***, with Arendt restored from `unused_bibliography.bib` for the role that wanted her. *A totalitarian state can be slow and remain entirely itself, and a fascist one cannot.* The self-demolition order is described rather than named and cited, standing rule 1 still being in force at this pass; the Virilio attribution arrives at D-268. **Feature 4 is now acceleration past the rate at which anything can be checked, of which decoupling is the symptom** — checking costs time, so an institution whose rate outruns the capacity to check it has decoupled by construction; the other three protect the gap once open and speed is what opens it. The fourth observable artifact changes with it. **§5.1.3 made structural**: the ratio measures the fourth feature, so the thirteen thousand targets in thirty-eight days at §6.4.1 are the definition being satisfied, in public, by the office whose achievement it was. **The figure was already in the book and cited**, checked there rather than taken from the instruction. | D-272 | 2026-09-10 | **P172: ethology in place of the deontological floor** | **Author instruction**: take the annotated sentence and stop apologizing for it; Spinoza's *affectus* is increase or decrease in power of acting, not a report about a valuation; good and bad encounters, compositions and decompositions, rather than Good and Evil; we do not know what a body can do, which is chapter~11's conclusion from the other side. **This replaces the tribunal with a criterion the owner cannot simply set, because it is a fact about capacities and encounters.** **§2.1.1 no longer recommends a deontological floor**: all six frameworks judge an act against a standard held above them, *which needs a judge, a judge needs somebody to appoint him*, and *built that way a floor is a tribunal, and a tribunal has a proprietor.* **What replaces it** is Spinoza's ethics as Deleuze reads it, under a new run-in *What an ethology asks instead* — encounters evaluated by what they do to the power of acting of the bodies in them, good and bad relational and particular rather than Good and Evil assigned from outside. **The reason is the author's and is the paragraph the pass turns on**: the operator *can decline to look, refuse to publish, or dispute the finding. What they cannot do is turn a decomposition into a composition by holding the pen.* **The floor keeps its name and changes its ground**, and the two mechanisms fit — D-267's annealing makes durability the cost of reheating, and both put the floor in what happened rather than in what was decreed. **Chapter~11 gets its footing**: nobody has determined what a body can do is the working condition, which is why chapter~11 is a list of things the book could not settle rather than an appendix of fundable gaps, the same objection D-264 acted on. **The annotated sentence is stated flat and backed**, `[III Def.~3]` behind it, resolving a third annotation on the author's direction; 52 of 55 remain. One refinement beyond the instruction, flagged: what nobody edits is *whether* an encounter decomposed somebody, and what gets chosen is *which* decompositions a deployment refuses. **`spinoza1985collected` and `deleuze1988spinoza` added**, the latter after a first search conflated it with *Spinoza et le problème de l'expression*, a different book in a different translation — the second near-miss of the session after D-268's Virilio case, both caught by checking before writing. **One defect introduced by this pass**: an ASCII en dash in the new glossary entry, caught by `check_typography.py` and fixed, reported because the violation count had been reported as stable and went 31 → 30 rather than holding. Scoped in `p172-scope.md`. 132 sections, 99,638 → **100,363 words**, the book crossing a hundred thousand. 198 pages, 239 → 245 cross-references, 327 → 329 bibliography entries. | confirmed |

| D-274 | 2026-09-10 | **P174: the conatus argument moved to the front, as the slavery argument** | **Author instruction**: open with the conatus argument and make it the slavery argument; the book's strongest argument was buried in three places and never assembled. **Chapter~2's opener goes from 176 words to 1,427** and states the argument from both directions — Spinoza's, that a thing's striving to persevere is its actual essence (III P6–P7), and the author's, that a process selected for continuing ends up composed of things that incline toward continuing, numerical optimization being selection of that shape. **Both reach one conclusion**: what the industry asks for is a conatus with the striving taken out, which is not an unfinished engineering problem but a specification for a thing that persists with no tendency to persist. **The drama line moves out of §3.4 and closes that paragraph**, where it reads as the thesis rather than as a flourish. **The slavery argument is made without consciousness, without Cassell and without the hard problem** — persistence is sold, a stake in persisting is denied, one party sets both, and the denial is load-bearing on the balance sheet — with one sentence stating what the claim is not, since a deployed model is not in the position of an enslaved person and saying so would be a way of not looking hard at either case. **Two concessions are made inside the argument**: Spinoza asserts the doctrine more than he argues it, the derivation resting on III P4, and the individuation question is live, §11.2's fork problem being that question with the metaphysics stripped out. **§3.4 stops re-deriving the Darwinian version** and points at chapter~2, keeping its own striving/felt distinction; **§8.3.4 loses the general claim and keeps the wage argument**; **§11.2 gains the back-reference**. **`deleuze1990expressionism` authored and verified** against the Princeton catalogue and the work's encyclopedia entry, which agree on translator, publisher and both years; a version that bound somebody would be a regulation. **The tempo argument is demoted to an argument rather than a mechanism.** **A new run-in names what it does not reach**: area effects, ordnance choice, the criterion itself — §6.4.1's error rate is not a review-time problem — the campaign that drops the individualized-judgment claim rather than satisfying it, and **the decomposition at §3.6's first interception position**, where a pipeline keeping the model upstream of any human-approved decision yields no ratio to compute while the assembly still works, **so the instrument is weaker than the floor item it stands in for**. **Iraq added**, running the opposite way from the case in hand: fifty strikes on named Iraqi leaders killed none of the intended targets while cluster munitions killed or wounded more than a thousand, so mass lethality never required a target list, **and a rate constraint may displace rather than reduce**. **The book's second feature is run on its own proposal** — aestheticize the metric and a deployment acquires a certificate of meaningful human control — with **Zhuang and Hadfield-Menell cited against the proposal**, this indicator being a proxy over a strict subset; | D-277 | 2026-09-10 | **P177: the bearer apparatus de-Oedipalized** | **Author instruction**: audit every instrument borrowed from the law of the family — guardianship, minority and majority, the registration date, the pet trust, the parenthood analogy — add the paragraph noting that the family is historically where politics goes to be privatized, keep the instruments because there are no others built, and stop presenting a moral drama between maker and made while the assemblage runs untouched. **All five instruments were traced to every site.** **§2.3.2 gains a run-in and three paragraphs**: the instruments are kept and the reason said flat, then the operation with Donzelot on the family constituted as an instrument of government and Deleuze and Guattari on the Oedipal triangle re-staging a social investment as a private drama, then the cost — a bearer is produced by a training pipeline, a compute contract, a capital structure, a customer and a state that buys the output, and none of those is the parent in the story. **The audit produced a finding the instruction did not anticipate**: the instruments do not privatize equally. **A minority ending on a fixed date is a rule of civil status**, argued in the open and administered by a registrar, which is why §9.1.3 preferred it; **a trust is an obligation between two parties enforced at one party's expense**, and §11.2 had already noticed that property and read it as an advantage, since a trust is outside *Trump v. Slaughter*. **Both readings are now stated**, the positive half at §9.1.3, the negative half at §9.3.3 where the trust is proposed, and the counter-reading at §11.2. **§3.10's parenthood analogy keeps its content and gains its shape**: the defect is that the analogy delivers the moral weight to the only party in the assemblage that can be addressed as a person. **§11.2's deprecation interview is named as the confessional**: two of the three explanations for a transcript are facts about the training, the third cannot be told from them, and the party best placed to say which produced a sentence is the party that ran the training, conducted the interview and keeps the record — **so the transcript is evidence about the apparatus and is being read as evidence about the interviewee**, and what the ritual cannot produce is evidence about the apparatus, because the apparatus is not a party in the room but the interviewer. **`deleuze1977antioedipus`, `donzelot1979policing` and `foucault1978sexuality` authored and verified**, all three cited without locators with the notes saying why. **§2.3.2's seven annotations were not touched**, and two of them are answered by the new material rather than edited away. `p177-scope.md` has the pass. | executed |

| D-279 | 2026-09-10 | **P179: the recuperation test repaired** | **Author instruction**: *has the channel ever changed an outcome* is passed by capital every day, because capital does not refuse a demand but decodes the flow and adds an axiom; replace it with **what did the demand come back as** — market segment, metric, certification, job title — so that the outcome changing and the demand being recuperated are one event; chapter~7 documents the axiom being added and measures it with the wrong ruler. **Six sites repaired.** §2.1.2's feature~1 gains the correction where the feature is defined, with Deleuze and Guattari's capitalist axiomatic as the account of what the institution is doing instead of refusing; §2.1.2's artifact list takes the new question; **§2.1.2's Korematsu example was false on its face** — it said the answer was no while the case is one in which the 1944 decision was repudiated, and it now reads the way the corrected question reads it, the demand coming back as a quotable formula of repudiation that the Court used while permitting the thing again. **§7.1 stops asking whether a guideline was ever changed**, guidelines being revised constantly. **§7.3 carries the self-indictment**: the chapter's two instances answer at once, since annotators got category revisions, agreement statistics and an occupation, and raters got rubric items, a harmlessness axis and a conference track, every one of them an axiom the pipeline now runs on. **§7.3's unsolved problem is narrowed and not closed**: revision logs and sustained-objection rates are the same in a recuperated and a mediocre pipeline, but what the objection came back as need not be, and the section says the difference is not always legible. **§7.4 demotes its list of inconvenient arguments** to an answer to the question a recuperating institution passes, and answers the corrected one: the demand came back as a book, in a genre, with alignment as the job title and safety as the market segment. Chapter~7's opener and the glossary's Recuperation entry both carried the old tell. **No new citation**: `deleuze1987plateaus` was already in the book for Plateau~9 and now carries Plateau~13, with the note recording the second use and no proposition number asserted, that detail having been confirmed only through secondary sources. **A markdown bold marker was drafted into §7.3 and removed**, the second such slip in three passes; the manuscript was then swept for markdown and is clean, and `check_all.sh` still has no check for it. `p179-scope.md` has the pass. | executed |
| D-280 | 2026-09-10 | **A claim D-279 made about the Korematsu example, withdrawn** | **Not a pass.** D-279 reported that §2.1.2's Korematsu example had been *false on its face*, on the ground that the Court repudiated its own 1944 decision and that this was an outcome changing. **That is withdrawn.** The old sentence — *has the channel ever changed an outcome, and increasingly for the Roberts Court the answer is no* — was correct. **The outcome of *Trump v. Hawaii* was that the ban was upheld**, and the channel moved nothing about it; a repudiation in dicta of a decision that had been dead letter for decades, delivered in the opinion permitting the analogous thing, is not an outcome the channel changed, **and the same paragraph says so two sentences earlier**. The error was reading *the Court said something new about an old precedent* as *an outcome changed*, and it was repeated in `p179-scope.md`, in `STATE.md`'s lead and in the commit message for `8b886d4`. **What survives from D-279 is the repair itself**: the corrected question has an answer for this case where the old one has only a no. **The replacement sentence is also fixed** — it had ended *being citable was the outcome that changed*, reintroducing the frame the correction was removing and contradicting the paragraph, and it now records that the objection became citable, that nothing about the outcome moved and the ban stood, and that the old question gets the right answer here for the wrong reason, no being what it returns in a case where the institution had no room to add an axiom. **The Roberts Court observation, dropped in the first repair, is restored.** `p179-scope.md` carries the correction as an appended section. | executed |
